[Director 指令] [Patch — Round 2 必修] 以下 3 个 warning 和 1 个 info 必须在 Round 2 提交前全部修复：

**W1: Argmin "5 seeds, 12.4%" → "6 seeds, 12.3%"（第三次被标记）**
experiments.tex 三处全文搜索替换：
- "5 seeds" (argmin 相关) → "6 seeds"
- "12.4" (argmin mean) → "12.3"
- "2.9%" (argmin std) → "2.6%"
- "+5.1pp" → "+5.2pp"
同时检查 abstract.tex, introduction.tex, conclusion.tex 中是否有 "+5.1" 或 "12.4"。

**W2: Limitations "19.0%" → "17.5%"（第二次被标记）**
Limitations 中 "19.0% success on LIBERO-Object is far from deployment-ready" → "17.5% mean success on LIBERO-Object is far from deployment-ready"

**W3: 三处 -ing 分词短语结尾**
1. Introduction: "...produce near-identical samples, establishing an architectural applicability boundary." → "...produce near-identical samples. This establishes an architectural applicability boundary."
2. Related Work: "...with L2 argmin replacing a learned verifier." → "...uses L2 argmin instead of a learned verifier."
3. Experiments: "...confirming that conformal scores at K=10 are unreliable..." → "...which confirms that conformal scores at K=10 are unreliable..."

**I4: Limitations 添加 seed43 correction underperformance 披露**
在 Limitations "Seed variance" 条目中添加：
"On one seed (seed 43), the correction slightly decreases overall performance relative to argmin selection alone (16% vs 17%), although the six-seed mean remains significantly higher (17.5% vs 12.3%, p < 0.01)."

修复后提交 Round 2。

[Director 指令] [Info] p-value 归属确认：

p=0.0074 的 Fisher exact test 是 **obs_only vs argmin**（105/600 vs 74/600），不是 vs VLA-only。你的提请正确。

论文中使用 p=0.0074 时必须关联 "17.5% vs 12.3% (argmin)" 比较，不能关联 "17.5% vs 11.2% (VLA-only)"。

如果论文需要 obs_only vs VLA-only 的 p-value，需要单独计算（obs_only 105/600 vs VLA-only ~56/500）。但目前 obs_only vs argmin 的 p=0.0074 已经足够强，不必额外计算。

请确认 Conclusion 和其他章节中 p=0.0074 的归属正确。

_source line 2295 · 2026-05-18T11:17:21.677610+00:00_

[Director 指令] [Patch — Round 2 修复]

**1. 三处 -ing 分词短语（第二次被标记，必须修）：**

逐句改写：
- Introduction: "...produce near-identical samples, establishing an architectural applicability boundary." → "...produce near-identical samples. This establishes an architectural applicability boundary."
- Related Work: "...with L2 argmin replacing a learned verifier." → "...and uses L2 argmin instead of a learned verifier."
- Experiments Ablation: "...confirming that conformal scores at K=10 are unreliable..." → "...which confirms that conformal scores at K=10 are unreliable..."

**2. Argmin std: 2.9% → 2.6%**
6 seeds {12,17,13,12,10,10} sample std = 2.58% ≈ 2.6%。全文搜索 "2.9" (argmin 相关) 替换为 "2.6"。

**3. p-value 归属：暂用 Option A**
在 Agent 重新计算两组 Fisher test 之前，先将 Conclusion 改为 Option A：
"17.5±2.8% vs. 11.2±1.6% (VLA-only), p = 0.0074"
把 vs argmin 的 margin (+5.2pp) 单独说明，不附 p-value。

Agent 计算结果出来后如需调整会再发 patch。

修复后提交 Round 3。

31	% --- Para 5: Contributions ---
32	Our contributions are:
33	\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,nosep]
34	  \item A sample-select-correct pipeline for frozen flow-matching VLAs with component decomposition: correction (${\sim}$5\,pp) dominates selection (${\sim}$1\,pp), and the full pipeline yields +5.2\,pp over argmin selection ($p = 0.0074$, 6 seeds) on LIBERO-Object.
35	  \item A systematic conditioning and magnitude study showing that observation-only conditioning is robust across all tested bounds, while conformal uncertainty features are harmful.
36	  \item Applicability boundaries: multi-sample correction requires stochastic action diversity (absent in tokenized VLAs) and does not transfer to spatial reasoning tasks.
37	\end{enumerate}

46	
47	\paragraph{Statistical protocol.}
48	Table~\ref{tab:main} reports seed 42 (10 rollouts $\times$ 10 tasks = 100 episodes per suite).
49	For the primary comparison, we replicate across multiple seeds on LIBERO-Object: obs\_only ($\varepsilon{=}0.2$, 6 seeds) achieves mean $17.5 \pm 2.8$\%, VLA-only (5 seeds) achieves mean $11.2 \pm 1.6$\%, and Argmin (6 seeds) achieves mean $12.3 \pm 2.6$\%.
50	Pooled Fisher's exact test: obs\_only vs.\ Argmin yields $p = 0.0074$ (Cohen's $d = 1.21$).
51	All six obs\_only seeds exceed the VLA-only mean, confirming reproducible improvement despite per-seed variance.
52	Multi-seed VLA-mean evaluation (4 seeds, mean 12.5\%) confirms that averaging provides no consistent benefit over single-sample execution.
53	
54	
55	\subsection{Main Results}

80	\end{table}
81	
82	\paragraph{Object benchmark: correction helps.}
83	On LIBERO-Object, obs\_only at $\varepsilon{=}0.1$ achieves 19.0\% (seed 42), and at $\varepsilon{=}0.2$ yields 22.0\%.
84	Across six seeds ($\varepsilon{=}0.2$), the pipeline achieves mean $17.5 \pm 2.8$\%, versus VLA-only mean $11.2 \pm 1.6$\% (+6.3\,pp) and Argmin mean $12.3 \pm 2.6$\% (+5.2\,pp, $p = 0.0074$, pooled Fisher's exact test; Cohen's $d = 1.21$).
85	
86	At seed 42, all three baselines achieve 12.0\%: neither averaging nor selection alone helps, confirming that action diversity does not center around the correct action.
87	The obs\_only CRM breaks through this plateau by predicting corrective residuals conditioned on the observation embedding.
88	
89	\paragraph{Pipeline decomposition.}
90	At $K{=}1$ (correction without selection), CRM achieves 10.3\% (3-seed mean), at the VLA-only level.
91	Argmin selection alone yields 12.3\% (6-seed mean).
92	Neither component alone improves the baseline, yet the full pipeline ($K{=}10$) achieves 17.5\% (vs.\ Argmin 12.3\%, $p = 0.0074$): selection provides a higher-quality starting point, and the CRM applies corrections that are more reliable on pre-screened candidates.
93	Correction accounts for ${\sim}$5\,pp and selection for ${\sim}$1\,pp.
94

_source line 2313 · 2026-05-18T11:20:00.775843+00:00_

33	\section{Conclusion}
34	\label{sec:conclusion}
35	
36	We presented a sample-then-correct pipeline that wraps a frozen flow-matching VLA with $L_2$ argmin selection and a lightweight residual correction module (${\sim}$327K parameters, ${\sim}$0.3\,ms overhead).
37	On LIBERO-Object, the mean margin over selection alone is +5.2\,pp (pooled: $17.5 \pm 2.8$\% vs.\ $12.3$\%, $p = 0.0074$), though individual seeds range from $-1$\,pp to $+10$\,pp, reflecting the stochastic nature of the evaluation.
38	Correction contributes ${\sim}$5\,pp and selection ${\sim}$1\,pp.
39	On LIBERO-Spatial, correction provides no benefit over selection, bounding the scope to object-centric manipulation.
40	Our central negative result is that per-DOF conformal features do not improve correction over observation-only conditioning at the $K{=}10$ sample budget, a cautionary finding for uncertainty-conditioned action correction.
41
